([LauraFlorentin/skills-marketplace](https://github.com/LauraFlorentin/skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/ma-advisory/skills/workflow-chains) · ★1)
- **workflow-check**: Quiz the developer on technical decisions in the current work. Use when the user explicitly requests the Virtual Team check workflow or when this workflow is the next stage of an active Virtual Team pipeline. ([ovargas/virtual-team](https://github.com/ovargas/virtual-team/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-check) · ★13)
- **workflow-checkpoint-refactor**: Multi-phase refactoring with checkpoint files that survive context limits. Use when refactoring spans 10+ files, needs phased rollout, or risks running out of context mid-session.
